{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1792694412260,"sessionID":"ses_e9b19886fffekdMr0CN165K57r","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_00_fte01k99z437lre5gm5xjxjg","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/cahn-hilliard_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_mpi_r5/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp","content":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Multi-dimensional block decomposition of an interval [0, n) into p parts.\ninline size_t part_start(const size_t n, const int p, const int i) noexcept {\n    const size_t pi = static_cast<size_t>(p);\n    const size_t base = n / pi;\n    const size_t rem = n % pi;\n    const size_t ii = static_cast<size_t>(i);\n    return ii * base + std::min(ii, rem);\n}\n\ninline size_t part_count(const size_t n, const int p, const int i) noexcept {\n    const size_t pi = static_cast<size_t>(p);\n    const size_t base = n / pi;\n    const size_t rem = n % pi;\n    return base + (static_cast<size_t>(i) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\nstruct Domain {\n    size_t nx = 0, ny = 0, nz = 0;\n    int dims[3] = {1, 1, 1};\n    int coords[3] = {0, 0, 0};\n    size_t lnx = 0, lny = 0, lnz = 0;\n    size_t ox = 0, oy = 0, oz = 0;\n    size_t pnx = 2, pny = 2, pnz = 2;\n    bool active = false;\n    int nbrLow[3] = {MPI_PROC_NULL, MPI_PROC_NULL, MPI_PROC_NULL};\n    int nbrHigh[3] = {MPI_PROC_NULL, MPI_PROC_NULL, MPI_PROC_NULL};\n};\n\n// Pick a 3D process grid.  Prefer grids whose extents do not exceed the\n// problem size, then balance local work and minimize halo surface area.\nstatic void chooseDims(const int nprocs, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, int dims[3]) {\n    int best[3] = {1, 1, nprocs};\n    long long bestActive = -1;\n    double bestCost = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n\n    for (int px = 1; px <= nprocs; ++px) {\n        if (nprocs % px != 0) continue;\n        const int remx = nprocs / px;\n        for (int py = 1; py <= remx; ++py) {\n            if (remx % py != 0) continue;\n            const int pz = remx / py;\n\n            const long long ax = std::min<long long>(px, static_cast<long long>(nx));\n            const long long ay = std::min<long long>(py, static_cast<long long>(ny));\n            const long long az = std::min<long long>(pz, static_cast<long long>(nz));\n            const long long active = ax * ay * az;\n\n            const double mx = static_cast<double>((nx + static_cast<size_t>(px) - 1) / static_cast<size_t>(px));\n            const double my = static_cast<double>((ny + static_cast<size_t>(py) - 1) / static_cast<size_t>(py));\n            const double mz = static_cast<double>((nz + static_cast<size_t>(pz) - 1) / static_cast<size_t>(pz));\n\n            const double volume = mx * my * mz;\n            const double surface = mx * my + my * mz + mx * mz;\n            const double cost = volume + surface;\n\n            if (active > bestActive || (active == bestActive && cost < bestCost)) {\n                bestActive = active;\n                bestCost = cost;\n                best[0] = px;\n                best[1] = py;\n                best[2] = pz;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    dims[0] = best[0];\n    dims[1] = best[1];\n    dims[2] = best[2];\n}\n\nstatic void setupNeighbors(Domain& d, const MPI_Comm cart) {\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        int src = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n        int dst = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n        MPI_Cart_shift(cart, dim, 1, &src, &dst);\n\n        const size_t nlen = (dim == 0) ? d.nx : (dim == 1) ? d.ny : d.nz;\n\n        int low = src;\n        int high = dst;\n\n        // A neighbor whose block is empty is not a real data source/sink.\n        // Treat it like a physical (clamped) boundary so that no rank waits\n        // on a message that will never be posted by an idle rank.\n        if (low != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            const int nc = d.coords[dim] - 1;\n            if (nc < 0 || static_cast<size_t>(nc) >= nlen) low = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n        }\n        if (high != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            const int nc = d.coords[dim] + 1;\n            if (nc < 0 || static_cast<size_t>(nc) >= nlen) high = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n        }\n\n        d.nbrLow[dim] = low;\n        d.nbrHigh[dim] = high;\n    }\n}\n\n// Datatypes describing one halo face with the padded local layout.  The\n// face normal is the dimension index: 0 = x, 1 = y, 2 = z.\nstatic void createHaloTypes(const Domain& d, MPI_Datatype types[3]) {\n    MPI_Datatype tmp;\n\n    MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(d.lny), 1, static_cast<int>(d.pnx), MPI_DOUBLE, &tmp);\n    MPI_Type_create_hvector(static_cast<int>(d.lnz), 1,\n                            static_cast<MPI_Aint>(d.pnx) * static_cast<MPI_Aint>(d.pny) *\n                                static_cast<MPI_Aint>(sizeof(double)),\n                            tmp, &types[0]);\n    MPI_Type_commit(&types[0]);\n    MPI_Type_free(&tmp);\n\n    MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(d.lnz), static_cast<int>(d.lnx),\n                    static_cast<int>(d.pnx * d.pny), MPI_DOUBLE, &types[1]);\n    MPI_Type_commit(&types[1]);\n\n    MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(d.lny), static_cast<int>(d.lnx),\n                    static_cast<int>(d.pnx), MPI_DOUBLE, &types[2]);\n    MPI_Type_commit(&types[2]);\n}\n\n// Exchange the six face halos with non-blocking point-to-point operations.\n// Physical boundaries (and empty neighbor blocks) are filled by clamped\n// copies of the adjacent interior plane.\nstatic void haloExchange(std::vector<double>& a, const Domain& d,\n                         const MPI_Datatype types[3], const MPI_Comm cart) {\n    if (!d.active) return;\n\n    const size_t pnx = d.pnx;\n    const size_t plane = d.pnx * d.pny;\n    auto P = [&](const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) -> size_t {\n        return z * plane + y * pnx + x;\n    };\n\n    MPI_Request reqs[12];\n    int nreq = 0;\n\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        size_t hLow, hHigh, iLow, iHigh;\n        if (dim == 0) {\n            hLow = P(0, 1, 1);\n            hHigh = P(d.lnx + 1, 1, 1);\n            iLow = P(1, 1, 1);\n            iHigh = P(d.lnx, 1, 1);\n        } else if (dim == 1) {\n            hLow = P(1, 0, 1);\n            hHigh = P(1, d.lny + 1, 1);\n            iLow = P(1, 1, 1);\n            iHigh = P(1, d.lny, 1);\n        } else {\n            hLow = P(1, 1, 0);\n            hHigh = P(1, 1, d.lnz + 1);\n            iLow = P(1, 1, 1);\n            iHigh = P(1, 1, d.lnz);\n        }\n\n        const int low = d.nbrLow[dim];\n        if (low == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            if (dim == 0) {\n                for (size_t z = 1; z <= d.lnz; ++z)\n                    for (size_t y = 1; y <= d.lny; ++y)\n                        a[P(0, y, z)] = a[P(1, y, z)];\n            } else if (dim == 1) {\n                for (size_t z = 1; z <= d.lnz; ++z)\n                    for (size_t x = 1; x <= d.lnx; ++x)\n                        a[P(x, 0, z)] = a[P(x, 1, z)];\n            } else {\n                for (size_t y = 1; y <= d.lny; ++y)\n                    for (size_t x = 1; x <= d.lnx; ++x)\n                        a[P(x, y, 0)] = a[P(x, y, 1)];\n            }\n        } else {\n            MPI_Irecv(&a[hLow], 1, types[dim], low, 100 + dim, cart, &reqs[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(&a[iLow], 1, types[dim], low, 100 + dim, cart, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        }\n\n        const int high = d.nbrHigh[dim];\n        if (high == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            if (dim == 0) {\n                for (size_t z = 1; z <= d.lnz; ++z)\n                    for (size_t y = 1; y <= d.lny; ++y)\n                        a[P(d.lnx + 1, y, z)] = a[P(d.lnx, y, z)];\n            } else if (dim == 1) {\n                for (size_t z = 1; z <= d.lnz; ++z)\n                    for (size_t x = 1; x <= d.lnx; ++x)\n                        a[P(x, d.lny + 1, z)] = a[P(x, d.lny, z)];\n            } else {\n                for (size_t y = 1; y <= d.lny; ++y)\n                    for (size_t x = 1; x <= d.lnx; ++x)\n                        a[P(x, y, d.lnz + 1)] = a[P(x, y, d.lnz)];\n            }\n        } else {\n            MPI_Irecv(&a[hHigh], 1, types[dim], high, 200 + dim, cart, &reqs[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(&a[iHigh], 1, types[dim], high, 200 + dim, cart, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential on the local interior.\nstatic void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c,\n                                     double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                     const Domain& d,\n                                     const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                     const double gamma, const double e_AA,\n                                     const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    const size_t pnx = d.pnx;\n    const size_t plane = d.pnx * d.pny;\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < d.lnz; ++z) {\n        const size_t zbase = (z + 1) * plane;\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < d.lny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = zbase + (y + 1) * pnx + 1;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < d.lnx; ++x) {\n                const size_t i = base + x;\n                const double cv = c[i];\n\n                const double cxx = (c[i + 1] + c[i - 1] - 2.0 * cv) / dx2;\n                const double cyy = (c[i + pnx] + c[i - pnx] - 2.0 * cv) / dy2;\n                const double czz = (c[i + plane] + c[i - plane] - 2.0 * cv) / dz2;\n                const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n                mu[i] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                      + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                      - gamma * lap;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update on the local interior.\nstatic void cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew,\n                               const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                               const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                               const Domain& d,\n                               const double D, const double dt,\n                               const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    const size_t pnx = d.pnx;\n    const size_t plane = d.pnx * d.pny;\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < d.lnz; ++z) {\n        const size_t zbase = (z + 1) * plane;\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < d.lny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = zbase + (y + 1) * pnx + 1;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < d.lnx; ++x) {\n                const size_t i = base + x;\n\n                const double mxx = (mu[i + 1] + mu[i - 1] - 2.0 * mu[i]) / dx2;\n                const double myy = (mu[i + pnx] + mu[i - pnx] - 2.0 * mu[i]) / dy2;\n                const double mzz = (mu[i + plane] + mu[i - plane] - 2.0 * mu[i]) / dz2;\n                const double lap = mxx + myy + mzz;\n\n                cnew[i] = cold[i] + dt * D * lap;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize the local interior using exactly the same global indexing and\n// pseudo-random sequence as the serial version.\nstatic void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const Domain& d) {\n    const size_t vol = d.nx * d.ny * d.nz;\n    const size_t nxny = d.nx * d.ny;\n    const size_t pnx = d.pnx;\n    const size_t plane = d.pnx * d.pny;\n\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < d.lnz; ++z) {\n        const size_t gz = d.oz + z;\n        const size_t zbase = (z + 1) * plane;\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < d.lny; ++y) {\n            const size_t gy = d.oy + y;\n            const size_t gbase = gz * nxny + gy * d.nx + d.ox;\n            const size_t base = zbase + (y + 1) * pnx + 1;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < d.lnx; ++x) {\n                const size_t linear_id = gbase + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[base + x] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Reproduce the serial, global linear ordering on rank 0 only.  This path is\n// used for the optional result printing / validation and is not part of the\n// timed simulation loop.\nstatic void gatherGlobal(const std::vector<double>& local, const Domain& d,\n                         const MPI_Comm cart, const int rank, const int nprocs,\n                         std::vector<double>& global) {\n    const size_t pnx = d.pnx;\n    const size_t plane = d.pnx * displs.resize(static_cast<size_t>(nprocs));\n        long long total = 0;\n        for (int r = 0; r < nprocs; ++r) {\n            int c[3];\n            MPI_Cart_coords(cart, r, 3, c);\n So for low side, I need to receive from low neighbor (source) and send to low neighbor. But send to low neighbor means dest of a shift with disp=+1 would be high, not low. The low neighbor expects a receive from its high neighbor (me). So I must send to low neighbor. The rank ID for low neighbor is `src` (coordinate -1). Sending to src is correct (MPI rank src). There is no requirement that send destination equals MPI_Cart_shift's `dest`; we just use the rank number of the neighbor. px <= nprocs; ++px) {\n        if (nprocs % px != 0) continue;\n        const int remx = nprocs / px;\n        for (int py = 1; py <= remx; ++py) {\n            if (remx % py != 0) continue;\n            const int pz = remx / py;\n\n            const long long ax = std::min<long long>(px, static_cast<long long>(nx));\n            const long long ay = std::min<long long>(py, static_cast<long long>(ny));\n            const long long az = std::min<long long>(pz, static_cast<long long>(nz));\n            const long long active = ax * ay * az;\n\n            const double mx = static_cast<double>((nx + static_cast<size_t>(px) - 1) / static_cast<size_t>(px));\n            const double my = static_cast<double>((ny + static_cast<size_t>(py) - 1) / static_cast<size_t>(py));\n            const double mz = static_cast<double>((nz + static_cast<size_t>(pz) - 1) / static_cast<size_t>(pz));\n\n            const double volume = mx * my * mz;\n            const double surface = mx * my + my * mz + mx * mz;\n            const double cost = volume + surface;\n\n            if (active > bestActive || (active == bestActive && cost < bestCost)) {\n                bestActive = active;\n                bestCost = cost;\n                best[0] = px;\n                best[1] = py;\n                best[2] = pz;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    dims[0] = best[0];\n    dims[1] = best[1];\n    dims[2] = best[2];\n}\n\nstatic void setupNeighbors(Domain& d, const MPI_Comm cart) {\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        int src = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n        int dst = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n        MPI_Cart_shift(cart, dim, 1, &src, &dst);\n\n        const size_t nlen = (dim == 0) ? d.nx : (dim == 1) ? d.ny : d.nz;\n\n        int low = src;\n        int high = dst;\n\n        // A neighbor whose block is empty is not a real data source/sink.\n        // Treat it like a physical (clamped) boundary so that no rank waits\n        // on a message that will never be posted by an idle rank.\n        if (low != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            const int nc = d.coords[dim] - 1;\n            if (nc < 0 || static_cast<size_t>(nc) >= nlen) low = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n        }\n        if (high != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            const int nc = d.coords[dim] + 1;\n            if (nc < 0 || static_cast<size_t>(nc) >= nlen) high = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n        }\n\n        d.nbrLow[dim] = low;\n        d.nbrHigh[dim] = high;\n    }\n}\n\n// Datatypes describing one halo face with the padded local layout.  The\n// face normal is the dimension index: 0 = x, 1 = y, 2 = z.\nstatic void createHaloTypes(const Domain& d, MPI_Datatype types[3]) {\n    MPI_Datatype tmp;","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":120,"totalLines":634,"truncated":true}},"title":"home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/cahn-hilliard_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_mpi_r5/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp","time":{"start":1792695208767,"end":1792695208787}},"id":"prt_164f60931001fsr4azhujT4SRD","sessionID":"ses_e9b19886fffekdMr0CN165K57r","messageID":"msg_164f60553001zEi84l181TQtIG"}}

The key is that any rank adjacent to an inactive rank along dimension d has the inactive rank's coord along d >= n_d? Not necessarily if inactive due to a different dimension. Hmm! Example above: inactive rank (0,2,0) is inactive because y=2 >= ny=2. Adjacent along x: (1,2,0), also inactive. Adjacent along z: nz=64, pz=1 so none. Adjacent along y: (0,1,0) active, and the difference is in y where inactive rank's coord y=2 >= ny=2, so our high check catches it. Good. In general, if rank R is inactive because coord[d0] >= n_{d0}, then any active rank adjacent to R along some dimension d must have R as neighbor in direction d. If d != d0, then R's coord[d0] still >= n_{d0}, but the active rank S has same coord[d0] as R along dimension d (since neighbors along d share other coords). Then S would also have coord[d0] >= n_{d0}, making S inactive, contradiction. Therefore d must equal d0? Wait if d != d0, neighbors along d share coord[d0], so S would be inactive too. So any active rank adjacent to R must be adjacent along a dimension where R's coord is out of grid range, i.e., d=d0 (or one of the zero dims). Then our neighbor validity check using coord[d] catches it. Excellent. So no active-inactive communication mismatch. Good reasoning; our logic is sound.
